Player: I search behind the loose cellar stones for the smuggler cache.
Narration: The rough stones feel cool and gritty beneath your hands as you probe the tight space behind them. After a moment of focused effort, your fingers brush against something yielding—a small, tightly bundled canvas pouch tucked into the shadowed gap. You carefully pull it free, confirming this is likely the smuggler's cache.

Player: I eavesdrop on the meeting at the ruined tollhouse to learn who the contact serves.
Narration: From your concealed position within the ruins, you manage to catch snippets of hushed conversation between the figures gathered around a scarred wooden table. The voices are low and conspiratorial, making precise words difficult to capture, but one name echoes clearly through the murmuring crowd. The meeting appears focused on logistics, with maps spread across the table serving as a backdrop to the clandestine discussion. You feel a slight prickle of danger as one of the attendees glances sharply in your general direction, though they quickly divert their attention back to the proceedings.

Player: I return to Bran and Garran with the manifest proving Captain Voss is behind the attacks.
Narration: The heavy parchment, stamped with official seals, crinkles loudly as you hand it over to Bran. Narration: A wave of damp, earthy air rises from the cooperage cellar as you sweep your gaze across the stacked barrels and rough-hewn planks.
